  /**
   * 挂物族右键交互（Player.cs:32093-32121 → TE*.OnPlayerInteraction 1:1）：
   *  手持可放且未收藏 → 放入（单槽族 TryPlacing :83-100：占位物先弹出为掉落，
   *   再写入新物 stack=1，手持 -1；模特/帽架 TryFitting :680-725/:337-369：
   *   多叠放 1 克隆/单叠整槽交换，回写手持格）；
   *  否则 → 取出（单槽族 = OnPlayerInteraction else 分支 :134-151 →
   *   KillTile(fail:true) → KillTile_DropItems :63703 提前 return：存放物弹为
   *   世界掉落、方块保留；模特/帽架原版此时打开槽位 UI（BasicOpenCloseInteraction
   *   TileEntity.cs:250-285），本作无该 UI，最小等价 = 按原版槽位序取首个非空，
   *   空手优先回手持格（保留词缀），否则入包、装不下掉落）
   */
  private useFurnitureInteraction(tx: number, ty: number, kind: FurnitureKind) {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const [ax, ay] = furnitureAnchor(st, kind, tx, ty);
    const list = this.world.furnitureItems;
    const held = this.player.inv.heldItem();
    const vid = held ? slotVanillaId(held.id) : -1;
    if (held && vid > 0 && !held.favorited && fitsFurniture(kind, vid)) {
      if (kind === 'display_doll') {
        const r = dollPlace(list, ax, ay, held, vid);
        if (r) {
          if (r.swappedOut) {
            // 整槽交换（Utils.Swap）：换出物回手持格，取消收藏（TEDisplayDoll.cs:707）
            this.player.inv.slots[this.player.inv.selected] = { ...r.swappedOut, favorited: false };
          } else if (held.stack <= 0) {
            this.player.inv.slots[this.player.inv.selected] = null;
          }
          this.sfx.play('pickup'); // SoundID 7（TryFitting :698）
          this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
        }
      } else if (kind === 'hat_rack') {
        const r = hatRackPlace(list, ax, ay, held, vid);
        if (r) {
          if (r.swappedOut) this.player.inv.slots[this.player.inv.selected] = { ...r.swappedOut, favorited: false };
          else if (held.stack <= 0) this.player.inv.slots[this.player.inv.selected] = null;
          this.sfx.play('pickup'); // SoundID 7（TEHatRack.cs:351）
          this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
        }
      } else {
        const r = furniturePlaceSingle(list, kind, ax, ay, held, vid);
        if (r.ok) {
          // 占位旧物先弹出（TryPlacing :91-93 result.DropItem → 世界掉落）
          if (r.replaced) {
            this.spawnDrop(ax * TILE + 8, ay * TILE, ITEM_DEFS[r.replaced.id].key, r.replaced.stack, 0, -2);
          }
          this.player.inv.removeAt(this.player.inv.selected, 1);
          this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
        }
      }
      this.input.rightDown = false;
      return;
    }
    // 取出
    if (kind === 'display_doll' || kind === 'hat_rack') {
      const taken = furnitureTakeFirst(list, kind, ax, ay);
      if (taken) {
        const sel = this.player.inv.selected;
        if (!this.player.inv.slots[sel]) {
          this.player.inv.slots[sel] = { id: taken.item.id, stack: taken.item.stack, prefix: taken.item.prefix };
        } else {
          const left = this.player.inv.add(taken.item.id, taken.item.stack);
          if (left > 0) this.spawnDrop(ax * TILE + 8, ay * TILE, ITEM_DEFS[taken.item.id].key, left, 0, -2);
        }
        this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
      }
    } else {
      const popped = furniturePopSingle(list, kind, ax, ay);
      if (popped) {
        this.spawnDrop(ax * TILE + 8, ay * TILE, ITEM_DEFS[popped.id].key, popped.stack, 0, -2);
      }
    }
    this.input.rightDown = false;
  }

  private drawFurnitureItems(world: World, cam: Camera, viewW: number, viewH: number, z: number) {

    const vy0 = cam.y - viewH / 2 / z - 80, vy1 = cam.y + viewH / 2 / z + 64;

 "new_string": "/** 类别 → 放置判定分发（Game 侧单入口） */\nexport function fitsFurniture(kind: FurnitureKind, vid: number): boolean {\n  switch (kind) {\n    case 'item_frame': case 'display_jar': return fitsItemFrame(vid);\n    case 'weapon_rack': return fitsWeaponRack(vid);\n    case 'food_platter': return fitsFoodPlatter(vid);\n    case 'hat_rack': return fitsHatRack(vid);\n    case 'display_doll': return fitsDisplayDoll(vid);\n    // 锚桩无右键放入语义（TECritterAnchor 无 OnPlayerInteraction；FitsItem :48-51\n    // 仅 OnWorldLoaded 存档校验用，放置链由物品侧 createTile=724 结构性保证）\n    case 'critter_anchor': return false;\n  }\n}"

 "replace_all": false,
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/FurnitureItems.ts",
 "old_string": "/** 存档条目 → 运行时记录（mapId = 磁盘稳定 id → 内部 id；防御：未知 id 归 0 → 置空槽） */\nexport function furnitureFromSave(entries: FurnitureSaveEntry[] | undefined, mapId: (id: number) => number): FurnitureItemRecord[] {\n  if (!entries) return [];\n  const out: FurnitureItemRecord[] = [];\n  for (const e of entries) {\n    const n = furnitureSlotCount(e.kind);\n    const slots: FurnitureSlot[] = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n      const s = e.slots[i];\n      const mapped = s ? mapId(s.id) : 0;\n      if (s && mapped > 0) slots.push({ id: mapped, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix });\n      else slots.push(null);\n    }\n    out.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, kind: e.kind, slots });\n  }\n  return out;\n}",
 "new_string": "/** 存档条目 → 运行时记录（mapId = 磁盘稳定 id → 内部 id；防御：未知 id 归 0 → 置空槽） */\nexport function furnitureFromSave(entries: FurnitureSaveEntry[] | undefined, mapId: (id: number) => number): FurnitureItemRecord[] {\n  if (!entries) return [];\n  const out: FurnitureItemRecord[] = [];\n  for (const e of entries) {\n    const n = furnitureSlotCount(e.kind);\n    const slots: FurnitureSlot[] = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n      const s = e.slots[i];\n      const mapped = s ? mapId(s.id) : 0;\n      if (s && mapped > 0) slots.push({ id: mapped, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix });\n      else slots.push(null);\n    }\n    out.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, kind: e.kind, slots });\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// TECritterAnchor（tile 724 捕获小动物锚桩，1.4.4+）——TE 等价的专用存取\n//   原版链：放置捕获小动物物品（DefaultToCapturedCritter createTile=724）→\n//     TELeashedEntityAnchorWithItem.PlaceFromPlayerPlacementHook（:43-58）把手持物\n//     写入 TE（InsertItem :60-64 → RespawnLeashedEntity 生成拴绳小动物）；\n//     破坏 → KillTile_DropItems :63739-63744 DropItemForTileBreak（同击掉物+碎块，\n//     与物品框两段式 pop 不同），tile 本体无掉落（GetItemDrop case 723/724\n//     WorldGen.cs:66141-66145 break）。\n//   本仓等价：furnitureItems 里 kind='critter_anchor' 的单槽记录（持久化走\n//   serialize furniture 段对 kind 透传，无需改存档）；拴绳小动物本体\n//   （LeashedEntity 族 + Main.cs:17895/22163 消费）未实装 → GAP ③ 见 render/CritterCage.ts\n// ============================================================================\n\n/** 放置：写入手持捕获物（InsertItem 1:1）。锚桩 1×1，锚点 = 放置格本身 */\nexport function critterAnchorPlace(\n  records: FurnitureItemRecord[], x: number, y: number,\n  item: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number },\n): FurnitureItemRecord {\n  removeFurnitureAt(records, x, y);\n  const rec: FurnitureItemRecord = {\n    x, y, kind: 'critter_anchor',\n    slots: [{ id: item.id, stack: 1, prefix: item.prefix }],\n  };\n  records.push(rec);\n  return rec;\n}\n\n/** 命中格 → 锚桩记录（1×1 无归一） */\nexport function critterAnchorAt(records: FurnitureItemRecord[], x: number, y: number): FurnitureItemRecord | undefined {\n  return records.find((r) => r.x === x && r.y === y && r.kind === 'critter_anchor');\n}\n\n/**\n * 破坏：取出并存清空存物（DropItemForTileBreak :28-40——itemType>0 才掉）。\n * @returns 被掉落的存物（null = 空桩/无记录）；调用方随后清格 + removeFurnitureAt\n */\nexport function critterAnchorBreakDrop(records: FurnitureItemRecord[], x: number, y: number): FurnitureSlot {\n  const rec = critterAnchorAt(records, x, y);\n  if (!rec) return null;\n  const item = rec.slots[0];\n  rec.slots[0] = null;\n  return item;\n}"
}

 "new_string": "    // ★备案（未加专属 case，勿一刀切）：388/389 高门（TallGate）与 617 遗物座的\n    // TileObjectData 均 StyleHorizontal=false（变体竖排），default 的 X 向 style*fw*18\n    // 对它们是错的——当前生成/放置链无 placeStyle>0 实例不触发，一旦引入多变体\n    // 须按表补竖排 case（参考 15/79/139 的写法）\n    default: return [style * fw * 18, 0];\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * 多格 framed 物件【单格】帧（= Game.tryPlace vanilla-style 分支与放置测试共用）：\n *  样式基偏移（furnitureStyleBase）+ 格内 18px 列/行步长；旗帜 91 例外走\n *  SceneMetrics 逆写（tryPlace 原内联逻辑逐字提取，行为不变）。\n *  原版帧语义（TileObject.Place L74-102）：frameY 从顶行 0 向下递增，\n *  dx/dy 为物件内偏移（dx=0,dy=0 即顶行锚格）\n */\nexport function framedCellFrame(sheet: number, fw: number, style: number, dx: number, dy: number): [number, number] {\n  if (sheet === 91) {\n    let row = 0;\n    while (row < 3 && (style + 21 - 111 * row) * 18 >= 1998) row++;\n    return [Math.max(0, (style + 21 - 111 * row) * 18), row * 54 + dy * 18];\n  }\n  const [bx, by] = furnitureStyleBase(sheet, style, fw);\n  return [bx + dx * 18, by + dy * 18];\n}"
}

}

    // 右键轨道：(最优先,防 NPC 交谈拦截) → 附近宝箱 → NPC 交谈 → 交互
    //（Player.cs:32806-32812：右键轨道格 → LaunchMinecartHook——与手持物品无关，
    //  用"最佳矿车"（装备槽 > 手持 > 背包）；矿车是坐骑召唤物，不消耗）
    const type = this.world.store.get(tx, ty);
    if (inp.rightDown && !this.minecart && type === this.trackTile && this.trackTile !== 0) {
      // 无矿车族物品也上车（默认木质车 13，Player.cs:22401）
      if (this.mountMinecartAt(tx, ty, this.bestCartMount())) this.input.rightDown = false;
      return;
    }
    if (inp.rightDown) {
      // 抚摸宠物（Main.cs:37404：右键悬停宠物投射物 → PetAnimal → HandleSpecialEvent
      // 21 PET_THE_PET）。命中宠物/光宠（光标世界点 32px 内）+ 玩家交互距离门
      // （IsProjectileInteractableAndInInteractionRange :22874-22886 = 宠物格在
      // TileReachCheckSettings.Simple 射程内——inTileRange 同款盒）才消费右键——
      // 优先级高于地块交互（原版 petting 判定在 TileInteraction 之前）
      {
        const [wx, wy] = this.camera.screenToWorld(inp.mouseX, inp.mouseY);
        const pet = [this.petFollower, this.lightPetFollower].find(
          (f) => f && !f.dead && Math.hypot(f.cx - wx, f.cy - wy) < 32
          && this.inTileRange(Math.floor(f.cx / TILE), Math.floor(f.cy / TILE)));
        if (pet) {
          this.achievements.handleSpecialEvent(21,
            { statLifeMax: this.player.maxHp, statManaMax: this.player.maxMana });
          this.spawnParticles(pet.cx, pet.y, '#FF90B0', 4, 1.2, { life: 40 }); // 爱心尘近似
          this.sfx.play('menuTick', 1);
          this.input.rightDown = false;
        }
      }
      // 光标格本身是蜥蜴祭坛时跳过 ±1 格宝箱搜索——神庙宝箱全庙随机散布可能
      // 贴着祭坛，原版无 ±1 搜索（点到箱本体才开箱）；不跳过则右键祭坛变成开箱
      const cursorIsAltar = type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_237_lihzahrd_altar'];
      const nearChest = cursorIsAltar ? null : this.findChestNear(tx, ty);
      if (nearChest) {
        this.tryOpenChest(nearChest);
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      } else if (this.talkToNearbyNpc(tx, ty)) {
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      } else if (heldDef && viIdFromKey(heldDef.key) === 5289) {
        // 矿车升级套件（Item 5289 MinecartPowerup → Player.unlockedSuperCart = true）：
        // 永久解锁 SuperCart 档（runSpeed 20/accel 0.1/jump 5.15，Mount.cs:371-379），
        // 持久化于世界 flags；已解锁时不再消耗
        if (!this.superCartUnlocked()) {
          (this.world.flags as Record<string, boolean>).superCart = true;
          this.player.inv.removeAt(this.player.inv.selected, 1);
          this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
          this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SuperCartUnlocked'));
        }
        this.input.rightDown = false;
      } else if (heldDef && viIdFromKey(heldDef.key) === 43) {
        if (!this.world.clock.isDay) {

```
  const out: FurnitureItemRecord[] = [];
  for (const e of entries) {
    const n = furnitureSlotCount(e.kind);
    const slots: FurnitureSlot[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const s = e.slots[i];
      const mapped = s ? mapId(s.id) : 0;
      if (s && mapped > 0) slots.push({ id: mapped, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix });
      else slots.push(null);
    }
    out.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, kind: e.kind, slots });
  }
  return out;
}

// ============================================================================
// TECritterAnchor（tile 724 捕获小动物锚桩，1.4.4+）——TE 等价的专用存取
//   原版链：放置捕获小动物物品（DefaultToCapturedCritter createTile=724）→
//     TELeashedEntityAnchorWithItem.PlaceFromPlayerPlacementHook（:43-58）把手持物
//     写入 TE（InsertItem :60-64 → RespawnLeashedEntity 生成拴绳小动物）；
//     破坏 → KillTile_DropItems :63739-63744 DropItemForTileBreak（同击掉物+碎块，
//     与物品框两段式 pop 不同），tile 本体无掉落（GetItemDrop case 723/724
//     WorldGen.cs:66141-66145 break）。
//   本仓等价：furnitureItems 里 kind='critter_anchor' 的单槽记录（持久化走
//   serialize furniture 段对 kind 透传，无需改存档）；拴绳小动物本体
//   （LeashedEntity 族 + Main.cs:17895/22163 消费）未实装 → GAP ③ 见 render/CritterCage.ts
// ============================================================================

/** 放置：写入手持捕获物（InsertItem 1:1）。锚桩 1×1，锚点 = 放置格本身 */
export function critterAnchorPlace(
  records: FurnitureItemRecord[], x: number, y: number,
  item: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number },
): FurnitureItemRecord {
  removeFurnitureAt(records, x, y);
  const rec: FurnitureItemRecord = {
    x, y, kind: 'critter_anchor',
    slots: [{ id: item.id, stack: 1, prefix: item.prefix }],
  };
  records.push(rec);
  return rec;
}

/** 命中格 → 锚桩记录（1×1 无归一） */
export function critterAnchorAt(records: FurnitureItemRecord[], x: number, y: number): FurnitureItemRecord | undefined {
  return records.find((r) => r.x === x && r.y === y && r.kind === 'critter_anchor');
}

/**
 * 破坏：取出并存清空存物（DropItemForTileBreak :28-40——itemType>0 才掉）。
 * @returns 被掉落的存物（null = 空桩/无记录）；调用方随后清格 + removeFurnitureAt
 */
export function critterAnchorBreakDrop(records: FurnitureItemRecord[], x: number, y: number): FurnitureSlot {
  const rec = critterAnchorAt(records, x, y);
  if (!rec) return null;
  const item = rec.slots[0];
  rec.slots[0] = null;
  return item;
}

```
